});===== mobile/src/app/(dashboard)/exploitations/[id]/edit.tsx =====
import { useCallback, useState } from "react";
import {
  View,
  Text,
  TextInput,
  Image,
  Pressable,
  StyleSheet,
  ActivityIndicator,
  ScrollView,
  KeyboardAvoidingView,
  Platform,
  Alert,
} from "react-native";
import { SafeAreaView } from "react-native-safe-area-context";
import { useLocalSearchParams, useRouter, useFocusEffect } from "expo-router";
import * as Location from "expo-location";
import * as ImagePicker from "expo-image-picker";
import {
  getExploitationById,
  updateExploitation,
  uploadExploitationPhoto,
  type Exploitation,
} from "../../../../services/exploitationservice";
import { EXPLOITATION_TYPES } from "../../../../constants/exploitationTypes";

// ADAPTER : remplacez par la vraie baseURL utilisee dans services/api.ts
const API_ORIGIN = "http://192.168.1.12:3000";

export default function EditExploitationScreen() {
  const { id } = useLocalSearchParams<{ id: string }>();
  const exploitationId = Number(id);
  const router = useRouter();

  const [exploitation, setExploitation] = useState<Exploitation | null>(null);
  const [loadingData, setLoadingData] = useState(true);
  const [name, setName] = useState("");
  const [superficie, setSuperficie] = useState("");
  const [type, setType] = useState<"OVIN" | "CAPRIN" | "MIXTE">("OVIN");
  const [latitude, setLatitude] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [longitude, setLongitude] = useState<number | null>(null);

  const [loadingLocation, setLoadingLocation] = useState(false);
  const [uploadingPhoto, setUploadingPhoto] = useState(false);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useFocusEffect(
    useCallback(() => {
      let active = true;
      setLoadingData(true);
      getExploitationById(exploitationId).then((result) => {
        if (!active) return;
        if (result.success) {
          setExploitation(result.exploitation);
          setName(result.exploitation.name);
          setSuperficie(result.exploitation.superficie ?? "");
          setType(result.exploitation.type);
          setLatitude(
            result.exploitation.latitude ? Number(result.exploitation.latitude) : null
          );
          setLongitude(
            result.exploitation.longitude ? Number(result.exploitation.longitude) : null
          );
        } else {
          setError(result.message);
        }
        setLoadingData(false);
      });
      return () => {
        active = false;
      };
    }, [exploitationId])
  );

  async function handlePickPhoto() {
    const permission = await ImagePicker.requestMediaLibraryPermissionsAsync();
    if (!permission.granted) {
      Alert.alert(
        "Permission requise",
        "Autorisez l'acces aux photos pour changer la photo de l'exploitation."
      );
      return;
    }

    const result = await ImagePicker.launchImageLibraryAsync({
      mediaTypes: ["images"],
      quality: 0.7,
      allowsEditing: true,
      aspect: [16, 9],
    });

    if (result.canceled || !result.assets?.[0]) return;

    setUploadingPhoto(true);
    const uploadResult = await uploadExploitationPhoto(
      exploitationId,
      result.assets[0].uri
    );
    setUploadingPhoto(false);

    if (uploadResult.success) {
      setExploitation(uploadResult.exploitation);
    } else {
      Alert.alert("Erreur", uploadResult.message);
    }
  }

  async function handleUseCurrentLocation() {
    setLoadingLocation(true);
    try {
      const permission = await Location.requestForegroundPermissionsAsync();
      if (!permission.granted) {
        Alert.alert(
          "Permission requise",
          "Autorisez l'acces a la localisation pour mettre a jour les coordonnees GPS."
        );
        return;
      }
      const position = await Location.getCurrentPositionAsync({});
      setLatitude(position.coords.latitude);
      setLongitude(position.coords.longitude);
    } catch {
      Alert.alert("Erreur", "Impossible de recuperer la position actuelle.");
    } finally {
      setLoadingLocation(false);
    }
  }

  function validate(): string | null {
    if (name.trim().length < 2) return "Le nom doit faire au moins 2 caracteres.";
    if (superficie && Number.isNaN(Number(superficie)))
      return "La superficie doit etre un nombre.";
    return null;
  }

  async function handleSubmit() {
    const validationError = validate();
    if (validationError) {
      setError(validationError);
      return;
    }

    setSaving(true);
    setError(null);

    const result = await updateExploitation(exploitationId, {
      name: name.trim(),
      superficie: superficie ? Number(superficie) : undefined,
      latitude: latitude ?? undefined,
      longitude: longitude ?? undefined,
      type,
    });

    setSaving(false);

    if (result.success) {
      router.replace({
        pathname: "/exploitations/[id]",
        params: { id: String(exploitationId) },
      } as any);
    } else {
      setError(result.message);
    }
  }

  if (loadingData) {
    return (
      <SafeAreaView style={styles.safeArea} edges={["top", "bottom"]}>
        <ActivityIndicator style={{ marginTop: 40 }} />
      </SafeAreaView>
    );
  }

  const typeInfo =
    EXPLOITATION_TYPES.find((t) => t.id === type) ?? EXPLOITATION_TYPES[0];

  return (
    <SafeAreaView style={styles.safeArea} edges={["top", "bottom"]}>
      <KeyboardAvoidingView
        style={{ flex: 1 }}
        behavior={Platform.OS === "ios" ? "padding" : undefined}
      >
        <View style={styles.header}>
          <Pressable onPress={() => router.back()} style={styles.backButton} hitSlop={12}>
            <Text style={styles.backButtonText}>‹</Text>
          </Pressable>
          <Text style={styles.headerTitle}>Modifier la fiche</Text>
          <View style={{ width: 32 }} />
        </View>

        <ScrollView
          contentContainerStyle={styles.container}
          keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled"
        >
          <Pressable onPress={handlePickPhoto} style={styles.photoWrapper}>
            {exploitation?.photo ? (
              <Image
                source={{ uri: `${API_ORIGIN}${exploitation.photo}` }}
                style={styles.photo}
              />
            ) : (
              <View style={[styles.photo, styles.photoPlaceholder]}>
                <Text style={styles.photoPlaceholderIcon}>{typeInfo.icon}</Text>
              </View>
            )}
            <View style={styles.photoBadge}>
              {uploadingPhoto ? (
                <ActivityIndicator size="small" color="#fff" />
              ) : (
                <Text style={styles.photoBadgeText}>Changer la photo</Text>
              )}
            </View>
          </Pressable>

          <View style={styles.fieldGroup}>
            <Text style={styles.label}>Nom de l'exploitation</Text>
            <TextInput
              style={styles.input}
              value={name}
              onChangeText={setName}
            />
          </View>

          <View style={styles.fieldGroup}>
            <Text style={styles.label}>Superficie (ha)</Text>
            <TextInput
              style={styles.input}
              keyboardType="decimal-pad"
              value={superficie}
              onChangeText={setSuperficie}
            />
          </View>

          <View style={styles.fieldGroup}>
            <Text style={styles.label}>Type d'elevage</Text>
            <View style={styles.typeRow}>
              {EXPLOITATION_TYPES.map((t) => {
                const selected = type === t.id;
                return (
                  <Pressable
                    key={t.id}
                    onPress={() => setType(t.id)}
                    style={[
                      styles.typeChip,
                      selected && { backgroundColor: t.bg, borderColor: t.color },
                    ]}
                  >
                    <Text style={styles.typeChipIcon}>{t.icon}</Text>
                    <Text
                      style={[styles.typeChipLabel, selected && { color: t.color }]}
                    >
                      {t.label}
                    </Text>
                  </Pressable>
                );
              })}
            </View>
          </View>

          <View style={styles.fieldGroup}>
            <Text style={styles.label}>Coordonnees GPS</Text>
            <Pressable
              style={styles.locationButton}
              onPress={handleUseCurrentLocation}
              disabled={loadingLocation}
            >
              {loadingLocation ? (
                <ActivityIndicator color="#2563eb" />
              ) : (
                <Text style={styles.locationButtonText}>
                  📍 Mettre a jour avec ma position
                </Text>
              )}
            </Pressable>
            {latitude !== null && longitude !== null && (
              <Text style={styles.coordsText}>
                {latitude.toFixed(6)}, {longitude.toFixed(6)}
              </Text>
            )}
          </View>

          {error && <Text style={styles.error}>{error}</Text>}

          <Pressable style={styles.button} onPress={handleSubmit} disabled={saving}>
            {saving ? (
              <ActivityIndicator color="#fff" />
            ) : (
              <Text style={styles.buttonText}>Enregistrer</Text>
            )}
          </Pressable>
        </ScrollView>
      </KeyboardAvoidingView>
    </SafeAreaView>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  safeArea: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: "#f5f5f5" },
  header: {
    flexDirection: "row",
    alignItems: "center",
    justifyContent: "space-between",
    paddingHorizontal: 12,
    paddingBottom: 12,
  },
  backButton: { width: 32, height: 32, alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center" },
  backButtonText: { fontSize: 26, color: "#1a1a1a", fontWeight: "400" },
  headerTitle: { fontSize: 16, fontWeight: "700" },
  container: { padding: 20, paddingTop: 4, flexGrow: 1 },

  photoWrapper: { width: "100%", marginBottom: 18, borderRadius: 14, overflow: "hidden" },
  photo: { width: "100%", height: 150, backgroundColor: "#ddd" },

import { useCallback, useState } from "react";
import { ActivityIndicator, FlatList, Pressable, StyleSheet, Text, TextInput, View } from "react-native";
import { SafeAreaView } from "react-native-safe-area-context";
import { useFocusEffect, useLocalSearchParams, useRouter } from "expo-router";
import { createAgriculturalEvent, listAgriculturalEvents, type AgriculturalEvent } from "@/services/calendarService";
const TYPES = ["VACCINATION", "TRAITEMENT", "PESEE", "MISE_BAS"] as const;
export default function CalendarScreen() { const { id: exploitationId } = useLocalSearchParams<{ id: string }>(); const router = useRouter(); const [events, setEvents] = useState<AgriculturalEvent[]>([]); const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true); const [title, setTitle] = useState(""); const [type, setType] = useState<typeof TYPES[number]>("VACCINATION"); const [date, setDate] = useState(new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10)); const [gestation, setGestation] = useState(""); const [error, setError] = useState(""); const load = useCallback(async () => { const r = await listAgriculturalEvents(Number(exploitationId)); if (r.success) setEvents(r.data); setLoading(false); }, [exploitationId]); useFocusEffect(useCallback(() => { setLoading(true); void load(); }, [load])); async function add() { if (title.trim().length < 2) return setError("Indiquez un intitulé."); const r = await createAgriculturalEvent({ exploitationId: Number(exploitationId), title: title.trim(), type, eventDate: date, gestationWeek: gestation ? Number(gestation) : undefined }); if (!r.success) return setError(r.message); setTitle(""); setGestation(""); setError(""); await load(); } return <SafeAreaView style={s.safe}><View style={s.head}><Pressable onPress={() => router.back()}><Text style={s.back}>‹</Text></Pressable><Text style={s.title}>Calendrier agricole</Text><View /></View><View style={s.form}><TextInput style={s.input} placeholder="Événement (ex. Vaccin troupeau A)" value={title} onChangeText={setTitle}/><View style={s.types}>{TYPES.map(x=><Pressable key={x} onPress={()=>setType(x)} style={[s.type,type===x&&s.active]}><Text style={type===x?s.activeText:undefined}>{x.replace("_"," ")}</Text></Pressable>)}</View><TextInput style={s.input} value={date} onChangeText={setDate} placeholder="AAAA-MM-JJ"/><TextInput style={s.input} value={gestation} onChangeText={setGestation} placeholder="Semaine de gestation (mise bas)" keyboardType="number-pad"/>{error ? <Text style={s.error}>{error}</Text>:null}<Pressable style={s.add} onPress={add}><Text style={s.addText}>Ajouter au calendrier</Text></Pressable></View>{loading?<ActivityIndicator/>:<FlatList data={events} keyExtractor={x=>String(x.id)} contentContainerStyle={s.list} renderItem={({item})=><View style={s.card}><Text style={s.event}>{item.title}</Text><Text style={s.meta}>{item.type.replace("_"," ")} · {item.eventDate} · Semaine {item.weekOfYear}{item.gestationWeek?` · Gestation S${item.gestationWeek}`:""}</Text></View>}/>}</SafeAreaView>; }

===== mobile/src/app/(dashboard)/exploitations/[id]/index.tsx =====
import { useCallback, useState } from "react";
import {
  View,
  Text,
  Image,
  Pressable,
  StyleSheet,
  ActivityIndicator,
  ScrollView,
  Alert,
} from "react-native";
import { SafeAreaView } from "react-native-safe-area-context";
import { Link, useLocalSearchParams, useFocusEffect, useRouter } from "expo-router";
import { Feather } from "@expo/vector-icons";
import * as ImagePicker from "expo-image-picker";
import {
  getExploitationById,
  uploadExploitationPhoto,
  type Exploitation,
} from "../../../../services/exploitationservice";
import { getExploitationTypeInfo } from "../../../../constants/exploitationTypes";

// ADAPTER : remplacez par la vraie baseURL utilisee dans services/api.ts
const API_ORIGIN = "http://192.168.1.12:3000";

export default function ExploitationDetailScreen() {
  const { id } = useLocalSearchParams<{ id: string }>();
  const exploitationId = Number(id);
  const router = useRouter();

  const [exploitation, setExploitation] = useState<Exploitation | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [uploadingPhoto, setUploadingPhoto] = useState(false);

  async function fetchExploitation() {
    setError(null);
    const result = await getExploitationById(exploitationId);
    if (result.success) {
      setExploitation(result.exploitation);
    } else {
      setError(result.message);
    }
  }

  useFocusEffect(
    useCallback(() => {
      setLoading(true);
      fetchExploitation().finally(() => setLoading(false));
    }, [exploitationId])
  );

  async function handlePickPhoto() {
    const permission = await ImagePicker.requestMediaLibraryPermissionsAsync();
    if (!permission.granted) {
      Alert.alert(
        "Permission requise",
        "Autorisez l'acces aux photos pour changer la photo de l'exploitation."
      );
      return;
    }

    const result = await ImagePicker.launchImageLibraryAsync({
      mediaTypes: ["images"],
      quality: 0.7,
      allowsEditing: true,
      aspect: [16, 9],
    });

    if (result.canceled || !result.assets?.[0]) return;

    setUploadingPhoto(true);
    const uploadResult = await uploadExploitationPhoto(
      exploitationId,
      result.assets[0].uri
    );
    setUploadingPhoto(false);

    if (uploadResult.success) {
      setExploitation(uploadResult.exploitation);
    } else {
      Alert.alert("Erreur", uploadResult.message);
    }
  }

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <SafeAreaView style={styles.safeArea} edges={["top", "bottom"]}>
        <ActivityIndicator style={{ marginTop: 40 }} />
      </SafeAreaView>
    );
  }

  if (error || !exploitation) {
    return (
      <SafeAreaView style={styles.safeArea} edges={["top", "bottom"]}>
        <View style={styles.center}>
          <Text style={styles.error}>{error ?? "Exploitation introuvable."}</Text>
        </View>
      </SafeAreaView>
    );
  }

  const typeInfo = getExploitationTypeInfo(exploitation.type);
  const hasCoords = exploitation.latitude && exploitation.longitude;

  return (
    <SafeAreaView style={styles.safeArea} edges={["top", "bottom"]}>
      <View style={styles.header}>
        <Pressable onPress={() => router.back()} style={styles.backButton} hitSlop={12}>
          <Text style={styles.backButtonText}>‹</Text>
        </Pressable>
        <Text style={styles.headerTitle}>Fiche exploitation</Text>
        <Link href={{ pathname: "/exploitations/[id]/edit", params: { id: String(exploitation.id) } }} asChild>
          <Pressable style={styles.headerEditButton} hitSlop={8}>
            <Feather name="edit-2" size={17} color="#166534" />
            <Text style={styles.headerEditText}>Modifier</Text>
          </Pressable>
        </Link>
      </View>

      <ScrollView contentContainerStyle={styles.container}>
        <Pressable onPress={handlePickPhoto} style={styles.photoWrapper}>
          {exploitation.photo ? (
            <Image
              source={{ uri: `${API_ORIGIN}${exploitation.photo}` }}
              style={styles.photo}
            />
          ) : (
            <View style={[styles.photo, styles.photoPlaceholder]}>
              <Text style={styles.photoPlaceholderIcon}>{typeInfo.icon}</Text>
            </View>
          )}
          <View style={styles.photoBadge}>
            {uploadingPhoto ? (
              <ActivityIndicator size="small" color="#fff" />
            ) : (
              <Text style={styles.photoBadgeText}>Changer la photo</Text>
            )}
          </View>
        </Pressable>

        <Text style={styles.name}>{exploitation.name}</Text>

        <Text
          style={[
            styles.typePill,
            { color: typeInfo.color, backgroundColor: typeInfo.bg },
          ]}
        >
          {typeInfo.icon} {typeInfo.label}
        </Text>
        <View style={styles.quickActions}>
          <Text style={styles.sectionTitle}>Gérer l’exploitation</Text>
          <View style={styles.actionGrid}>
            <MenuAction href="/exploitations/[id]/dashboard" id={exploitation.id} icon="bar-chart-2" label="Tableau de bord" tint="#166534" background="#e9f7ee" />
            <MenuAction href="/exploitations/[id]/batiments" id={exploitation.id} icon="home" label="Bâtiments" tint="#a16207" background="#fef3c7" />
            <MenuAction href="/exploitations/[id]/teams" id={exploitation.id} icon="users" label="Équipes" tint="#0f766e" background="#ccfbf1" />
            <MenuAction href="/exploitations/[id]/calendar" id={exploitation.id} icon="calendar" label="Calendrier" tint="#6d28d9" background="#ede9fe" />
          </View>
        </View>

        <View style={styles.infoBlock}>
          <InfoRow
            label="Superficie"
            value={exploitation.superficie ? `${exploitation.superficie} ha` : "—"}
          />
          <InfoRow
            label="Coordonnees GPS"
            value={
              hasCoords
                ? `${Number(exploitation.latitude).toFixed(5)}, ${Number(
                    exploitation.longitude
                  ).toFixed(5)}`
                : "Non renseignees"
            }
          />
          <InfoRow
            label="Creee le"
            value={new Date(exploitation.createdAt).toLocaleDateString("fr-FR")}
            last
          />
        </View>
      </ScrollView>
    </SafeAreaView>
  );
}

function MenuAction({ href, id, icon, label, tint, background }: { href: string; id: number; icon: any; label: string; tint: string; background: string }) {
  return (
    <Link href={{ pathname: href as any, params: { id: String(id) } }} asChild>
      <Pressable style={styles.actionCard}>
        <View style={[styles.actionIcon, { backgroundColor: background }]}><Feather name={icon} size={22} color={tint} /></View>
        <Text style={styles.actionLabel}>{label}</Text>
        <Feather name="chevron-right" size={16} color="#94a3b8" />
      </Pressable>
    </Link>
  );
}
function InfoRow({
  label,
  value,
  last,
}: {
  label: string;
  value: string;
  last?: boolean;
}) {
  return (
    <View style={[styles.infoRow, last && { borderBottomWidth: 0 }]}>
      <Text style={styles.infoLabel}>{label}</Text>
      <Text style={styles.infoValue}>{value}</Text>
    </View>
  );
}

===== mobile/src/app/(dashboard)/exploitations/[id]/batiments/create.tsx =====
import { useState } from "react";
import {
  View,
  Text,
  TextInput,
  Pressable,
  StyleSheet,
  ActivityIndicator,
  ScrollView,
  KeyboardAvoidingView,
  Platform,
} from "react-native";
import { SafeAreaView } from "react-native-safe-area-context";
import { useLocalSearchParams, useRouter } from "expo-router";
import { createBatiment } from "../../../../../services/batimentService";
import { BATIMENT_TYPES, BATIMENT_ETATS } from "../../../../../constants/batimentTypes";
import EquipmentTagsInput from "../../../../../components/EquipmentTagsInput";

const ACCENT = "#166534";

export default function CreateBatimentScreen() {
  const { id } = useLocalSearchParams<{ id: string }>();
  const exploitationId = Number(id);
  const router = useRouter();

  const [name, setName] = useState("");
  const [type, setType] = useState<
    "BERGERIE" | "STABULATION" | "BOX" | "PARC" | "PARCELLE"
  >("BERGERIE");
  const [capacite, setCapacite] = useState("");
  const [superficie, setSuperficie] = useState("");
  const [equipements, setEquipements] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [etat, setEtat] = useState<"BON" | "MOYEN" | "MAUVAIS">("BON");
  const [occupationActuelle, setOccupationActuelle] = useState("");

  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const superficieUnit = type === "PARCELLE" ? "ha" : "m²";

  function validate(): string | null {
    if (name.trim().length < 2) return "Le nom doit faire au moins 2 caracteres.";
    if (capacite && Number.isNaN(Number(capacite)))
      return "La capacite doit etre un nombre.";
    if (superficie && Number.isNaN(Number(superficie)))
      return "La superficie doit etre un nombre.";
    if (occupationActuelle && Number.isNaN(Number(occupationActuelle)))
      return "L'occupation actuelle doit etre un nombre.";
    return null;
  }

  async function handleSubmit() {
    const validationError = validate();
    if (validationError) {
      setError(validationError);
      return;
    }

    setLoading(true);
    setError(null);

    const result = await createBatiment({
      exploitationId,
      name: name.trim(),
      type,
      capacite: capacite ? Number(capacite) : undefined,
      superficie: superficie ? Number(superficie) : undefined,
      equipements,
      etat,
      occupationActuelle: occupationActuelle ? Number(occupationActuelle) : 0,
    });

    setLoading(false);

    if (result.success) {
      router.back();
    } else {
      setError(result.message);
    }
  }

  return (
    <SafeAreaView style={styles.safeArea} edges={["top", "bottom"]}>
      <KeyboardAvoidingView
        style={{ flex: 1 }}
        behavior={Platform.OS === "ios" ? "padding" : undefined}
      >
        <View style={styles.header}>
          <Pressable onPress={() => router.back()} style={styles.backButton} hitSlop={12}>
            <Text style={styles.backButtonText}>‹</Text>
          </Pressable>
          <Text style={styles.headerTitle}>Nouveau batiment / parcelle</Text>
          <View style={{ width: 32 }} />
        </View>

        <ScrollView
          contentContainerStyle={styles.container}
          keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled"
        >
          <View style={styles.fieldGroup}>
            <Text style={styles.label}>Nom</Text>
            <TextInput
              style={styles.input}
              placeholder="Ex : Bergerie principale"
              placeholderTextColor="#aaa"
              value={name}
              onChangeText={setName}
            />
          </View>

          <View style={styles.fieldGroup}>
            <Text style={styles.label}>Type</Text>
            <View style={styles.chipsWrap}>
              {BATIMENT_TYPES.map((t) => {
                const selected = type === t.id;
                return (
                  <Pressable
                    key={t.id}
                    onPress={() => setType(t.id)}
                    style={[
                      styles.typeChip,
                      selected && { backgroundColor: t.bg, borderColor: t.color },
                    ]}
                  >
                    <Text style={styles.typeChipIcon}>{t.icon}</Text>
                    <Text
                      style={[styles.typeChipLabel, selected && { color: t.color }]}
                    >
                      {t.label}
                    </Text>
                  </Pressable>
                );
              })}
            </View>
          </View>

          <View style={styles.row}>
            <View style={[styles.fieldGroup, { flex: 1 }]}>
              <Text style={styles.label}>Capacite (têtes)</Text>
              <TextInput
                style={styles.input}
                placeholder="Optionnel"
                placeholderTextColor="#aaa"
                keyboardType="number-pad"
                value={capacite}
                onChangeText={setCapacite}
              />
            </View>
            <View style={[styles.fieldGroup, { flex: 1 }]}>
              <Text style={styles.label}>Superficie ({superficieUnit})</Text>
              <TextInput
                style={styles.input}
                placeholder="Optionnel"
                placeholderTextColor="#aaa"
                keyboardType="decimal-pad"
                value={superficie}
                onChangeText={setSuperficie}
              />
            </View>
          </View>

          <View style={styles.fieldGroup}>
            <Text style={styles.label}>Occupation actuelle (têtes)</Text>
            <TextInput
              style={styles.input}
              placeholder="0"
              placeholderTextColor="#aaa"
              keyboardType="number-pad"
              value={occupationActuelle}
              onChangeText={setOccupationActuelle}
            />
          </View>

          <View style={styles.fieldGroup}>
            <Text style={styles.label}>Etat</Text>
            <View style={styles.chipsWrap}>
              {BATIMENT_ETATS.map((e) => {
                const selected = etat === e.id;
                return (
                  <Pressable
                    key={e.id}
                    onPress={() => setEtat(e.id)}
                    style={[
                      styles.etatChip,
                      selected && { backgroundColor: e.bg, borderColor: e.color },
                    ]}
                  >
                    <Text
                      style={[styles.etatChipLabel, selected && { color: e.color }]}
                    >
                      {e.label}
                    </Text>
                  </Pressable>
                );
              })}
            </View>
          </View>

          <View style={styles.fieldGroup}>
            <Text style={styles.label}>Equipements</Text>
            <EquipmentTagsInput value={equipements} onChange={setEquipements} />
          </View>

          {error && <Text style={styles.error}>{error}</Text>}

          <Pressable style={styles.button} onPress={handleSubmit} disabled={loading}>
            {loading ? (
              <ActivityIndicator color="#fff" />
            ) : (
              <Text style={styles.buttonText}>Enregistrer</Text>
            )}
          </Pressable>
        </ScrollView>
      </KeyboardAvoidingView>
    </SafeAreaView>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  safeArea: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: "#f5f5f5" },
  header: {
    flexDirection: "row",
    alignItems: "center",
    justifyContent: "space-between",
    paddingHorizontal: 12,
    paddingBottom: 12,
  },
  backButton: { width: 32, height: 32, alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center" },
  backButtonText: { fontSize: 26, color: "#1a1a1a", fontWeight: "400" },
  headerTitle: { fontSize: 15, fontWeight: "700" },
  container: { padding: 20, paddingTop: 4, flexGrow: 1 },
  row: { flexDirection: "row", gap: 10 },

});===== mobile/src/app/(dashboard)/exploitations/[id]/batiments/index.tsx =====
import { useCallback, useState } from "react";
import {
  View,
  Text,
  FlatList,
  Pressable,
  StyleSheet,
  ActivityIndicator,
  RefreshControl,
} from "react-native";
import { SafeAreaView } from "react-native-safe-area-context";
import { Link, useFocusEffect, useLocalSearchParams, useRouter } from "expo-router";
import { listBatiments, type Batiment } from "../../../../../services/batimentService";
import {
  getBatimentTypeInfo,
  getBatimentEtatInfo,
} from "../../../../../constants/batimentTypes";

export default function BatimentsListScreen() {
  const { id } = useLocalSearchParams<{ id: string }>();
  const exploitationId = Number(id);
  const router = useRouter();

  const [batiments, setBatiments] = useState<Batiment[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [refreshing, setRefreshing] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  async function fetchBatiments() {
    setError(null);
    const result = await listBatiments({ exploitationId, limit: 100 });
    if (result.success) {
      setBatiments(result.data);
    } else {
      setError(result.message);
    }
  }

  useFocusEffect(
    useCallback(() => {
      setLoading(true);
      fetchBatiments().finally(() => setLoading(false));
    }, [exploitationId])
  );

  async function onRefresh() {
    setRefreshing(true);
    await fetchBatiments();
    setRefreshing(false);
  }

  return (
    <SafeAreaView style={styles.safeArea} edges={["top", "bottom"]}>
      <View style={styles.header}>
        <Pressable onPress={() => router.back()} style={styles.backButton} hitSlop={12}>
          <Text style={styles.backButtonText}>‹</Text>
        </Pressable>
        <Text style={styles.headerTitle}>Batiments & parcelles</Text>
        <View style={{ width: 32 }} />
      </View>

      <View style={styles.container}>
        <Text style={styles.subtitle}>
          {batiments.length} structure{batiments.length > 1 ? "s" : ""}
        </Text>

        {error && <Text style={styles.error}>{error}</Text>}

        {loading ? (
          <ActivityIndicator style={{ marginTop: 24 }} />
        ) : (
          <FlatList
            data={batiments}
            keyExtractor={(item) => String(item.id)}
            contentContainerStyle={styles.listContent}
            refreshControl={
              <RefreshControl refreshing={refreshing} onRefresh={onRefresh} />
            }
            ListEmptyComponent={
              <Text style={styles.empty}>Aucun batiment ou parcelle enregistre.</Text>
            }
            ListFooterComponent={
              <Link
                href={
                  {
                    pathname: "/exploitations/[id]/batiments/create",
                    params: { id: String(exploitationId) },
                  } as any
                }
                asChild
              >
                <Pressable style={styles.addCard}>
                  <View style={styles.addIconCircle}>
                    <Text style={styles.addIcon}>+</Text>
                  </View>
                  <Text style={styles.addCardText}>Ajouter un batiment / parcelle</Text>
                </Pressable>
              </Link>
            }
            renderItem={({ item }) => {
              const typeInfo = getBatimentTypeInfo(item.type);
              const etatInfo = getBatimentEtatInfo(item.etat);
              const occupationRate = item.capacite
                ? Math.round((item.occupationActuelle / item.capacite) * 100)
                : null;

              return (
                <Link
                  href={
                    {
                      pathname: "/exploitations/[id]/batiments/[batimentId]",
                      params: {
                        id: String(exploitationId),
                        batimentId: String(item.id),
                      },
                    } as any
                  }
                  asChild
                >
                  <Pressable style={styles.card}>
                    <View style={[styles.iconWrapper, { backgroundColor: typeInfo.bg }]}>
                      <Text style={styles.icon}>{typeInfo.icon}</Text>
                    </View>

                    <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
                      <Text style={styles.name}>{item.name}</Text>
                      <Text style={styles.meta}>
                        {typeInfo.label}
                        {item.superficie ? ` · ${item.superficie} ${
                          item.type === "PARCELLE" ? "ha" : "m²"
                        }` : ""}
                      </Text>

                      <View style={styles.pillsRow}>
                        <Text
                          style={[
                            styles.etatPill,
                            { color: etatInfo.color, backgroundColor: etatInfo.bg },
                          ]}
                        >
                          {etatInfo.label}
                        </Text>
                        {item.capacite !== null && (
                          <Text style={styles.occupationText}>
                            {item.occupationActuelle}/{item.capacite}
                            {occupationRate !== null ? ` (${occupationRate}%)` : ""}
                          </Text>
                        )}
                      </View>
                    </View>

                    <Text style={styles.chevron}>›</Text>
                  </Pressable>
                </Link>
              );
            }}
          />
        )}
      </View>
    </SafeAreaView>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  safeArea: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: "#f5f5f5" },
  header: {

});===== mobile/src/app/(dashboard)/exploitations/[id]/batiments/[batimentId]/index.tsx =====
import { useCallback, useState } from "react";
import { ActivityIndicator, Alert, Pressable, ScrollView, StyleSheet, Text, View } from "react-native";
import { SafeAreaView } from "react-native-safe-area-context";
import { useFocusEffect, useLocalSearchParams, useRouter } from "expo-router";
import { deleteBatiment, getBatimentById, type Batiment } from "../../../../../../services/batimentService";
import { getBatimentEtatInfo, getBatimentTypeInfo } from "../../../../../../constants/batimentTypes";

export default function BatimentDetailScreen() {
  const { batimentId } = useLocalSearchParams<{ batimentId: string }>();
  const router = useRouter();
  const [batiment, setBatiment] = useState<Batiment | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const loadBatiment = useCallback(async () => {
    setError(null);
    const result = await getBatimentById(Number(batimentId));
    if (result.success) setBatiment(result.batiment);
    else setError(result.message);
  }, [batimentId]);

  useFocusEffect(
    useCallback(() => {
      setLoading(true);
      loadBatiment().finally(() => setLoading(false));
    }, [loadBatiment])
  );

  function confirmDelete() {
    if (!batiment) return;
    Alert.alert("Supprimer le bâtiment", `Supprimer « ${batiment.name} » ?`, [
      { text: "Annuler", style: "cancel" },
      {
        text: "Supprimer",
        style: "destructive",
        onPress: async () => {
          const result = await deleteBatiment(batiment.id);
          if (result.success) router.back();
          else Alert.alert("Erreur", result.message);
        },
      },
    ]);
  }

  if (loading) return <SafeAreaView style={styles.safeArea}><ActivityIndicator style={styles.loader} /></SafeAreaView>;
  if (error || !batiment) return <SafeAreaView style={styles.safeArea}><View style={styles.center}><Text style={styles.error}>{error ?? "Bâtiment introuvable."}</Text></View></SafeAreaView>;

  const type = getBatimentTypeInfo(batiment.type);
  const etat = getBatimentEtatInfo(batiment.etat);
  const occupationRate = batiment.capacite ? Math.round((batiment.occupationActuelle / batiment.capacite) * 100) : null;

  return (
    <SafeAreaView style={styles.safeArea} edges={["top", "bottom"]}>
      <View style={styles.header}>
        <Pressable onPress={() => router.back()}><Text style={styles.back}>‹</Text></Pressable>
        <Text style={styles.headerTitle}>Fiche bâtiment</Text>
        <View style={{ width: 18 }} />
      </View>
      <ScrollView contentContainerStyle={styles.container}>
        <Text style={styles.icon}>{type.icon}</Text>
        <Text style={styles.name}>{batiment.name}</Text>
        <Text style={[styles.type, { color: type.color, backgroundColor: type.bg }]}>{type.label}</Text>
        <View style={styles.card}>
          <InfoRow label="État" value={etat.label} valueStyle={{ color: etat.color }} />
          <InfoRow label="Capacité" value={batiment.capacite === null ? "Non renseignée" : `${batiment.capacite} têtes`} />
          <InfoRow label="Occupation" value={batiment.capacite === null ? `${batiment.occupationActuelle} têtes` : `${batiment.occupationActuelle}/${batiment.capacite} (${occupationRate}%)`} />
          <InfoRow label="Superficie" value={batiment.superficie ? `${batiment.superficie} ${batiment.type === "PARCELLE" ? "ha" : "m²"}` : "Non renseignée"} />
        </View>
        <Text style={styles.sectionTitle}>Équipements</Text>
        <View style={styles.equipmentRow}>
          {batiment.equipements.length ? batiment.equipements.map((item) => <Text key={item} style={styles.equipment}>{item}</Text>) : <Text style={styles.muted}>Aucun équipement renseigné.</Text>}
        </View>
        <Pressable style={styles.deleteButton} onPress={confirmDelete}><Text style={styles.deleteText}>Supprimer ce bâtiment</Text></Pressable>
      </ScrollView>
    </SafeAreaView>
  );
}

function InfoRow({ label, value, valueStyle }: { label: string; value: string; valueStyle?: object }) {
  return <View style={styles.row}><Text style={styles.label}>{label}</Text><Text style={[styles.value, valueStyle]}>{value}</Text></View>;
}

  safeArea: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: "#f5f5f5" }, loader: { marginTop: 40 }, center: { flex: 1, alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center" }, error: { color: "#dc2626" },

===== mobile/src/app/(dashboard)/exploitations/[id]/teams/create.tsx =====
import { useState } from "react";
import { ActivityIndicator, Pressable, ScrollView, StyleSheet, Text, TextInput, View } from "react-native";
import { SafeAreaView } from "react-native-safe-area-context";
import { useLocalSearchParams, useRouter } from "expo-router";
import { createContract, createEmployee } from "@/services/teamService";

export default function CreateEmployeeScreen() {
  const { id: exploitationId } = useLocalSearchParams<{ id: string }>(); const router = useRouter();
  const [firstName, setFirstName] = useState(""); const [lastName, setLastName] = useState(""); const [position, setPosition] = useState(""); const [phone, setPhone] = useState(""); const [hourlyRate, setHourlyRate] = useState(""); const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false); const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  async function submit() { if (firstName.trim().length < 2 || lastName.trim().length < 2 || position.trim().length < 2) { setError("Prénom, nom et fonction sont obligatoires."); return; } if (hourlyRate && Number.isNaN(Number(hourlyRate))) { setError("Le taux horaire est invalide."); return; } setLoading(true); setError(null); const employee = await createEmployee({ exploitationId: Number(exploitationId), firstName: firstName.trim(), lastName: lastName.trim(), position: position.trim(), phone: phone.trim() || undefined, status: "ACTIVE" }); if (!employee.success) { setError(employee.message); setLoading(false); return; } if (hourlyRate) { const contract = await createContract({ employeeId: employee.data.id, type: "CDI", startDate: new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10), hourlyRate: Number(hourlyRate) }); if (!contract.success) { setError(`Salarié créé, mais contrat non enregistré : ${contract.message}`); setLoading(false); return; } } setLoading(false); router.back(); }
  return <SafeAreaView style={styles.safe}><View style={styles.header}><Pressable onPress={() => router.back()}><Text style={styles.back}>‹</Text></Pressable><Text style={styles.title}>Nouveau salarié</Text><View style={{ width: 18 }} /></View><ScrollView contentContainerStyle={styles.container}><Field label="Prénom *" value={firstName} onChange={setFirstName} /><Field label="Nom *" value={lastName} onChange={setLastName} /><Field label="Fonction *" value={position} onChange={setPosition} placeholder="Ex. Berger" /><Field label="Téléphone" value={phone} onChange={setPhone} keyboard="phone-pad" /><Text style={styles.help}>Contrat initial (facultatif)</Text><Field label="Taux horaire (MAD)" value={hourlyRate} onChange={setHourlyRate} keyboard="decimal-pad" />{error && <Text style={styles.error}>{error}</Text>}<Pressable style={styles.button} onPress={submit} disabled={loading}>{loading ? <ActivityIndicator color="#fff" /> : <Text style={styles.buttonText}>Enregistrer</Text>}</Pressable></ScrollView></SafeAreaView>;
}
function Field({ label, value, onChange, placeholder, keyboard }: { label: string; value: string; onChange: (value: string) => void; placeholder?: string; keyboard?: "phone-pad" | "decimal-pad" }) { return <View style={styles.field}><Text style={styles.label}>{label}</Text><TextInput style={styles.input} value={value} onChangeText={onChange} placeholder={placeholder} keyboardType={keyboard} /></View>; }
const styles = StyleSheet.create({ safe: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: "#f8fafc" }, header: { flexDirection: "row", justifyContent: "space-between", alignItems: "center", paddingHorizontal: 16, paddingBottom: 10 }, back: { fontSize: 30 }, title: { fontSize: 16, fontWeight: "700" }, container: { padding: 20 }, field: { marginBottom: 14 }, label: { fontSize: 13, fontWeight: "600", color: "#374151", marginBottom: 6 }, input: { backgroundColor: "#fff", borderRadius: 10, borderWidth: 1, borderColor: "#d1d5db", padding: 12 }, help: { marginTop: 8, marginBottom: 12, fontWeight: "700", color: "#166534" }, error: { color: "#b91c1c", marginBottom: 12 }, button: { backgroundColor: "#166534", borderRadius: 10, padding: 15, alignItems: "center", marginTop: 8 }, buttonText: { color: "#fff", fontWeight: "700" } });
===== mobile/src/app/(dashboard)/exploitations/[id]/teams/index.tsx =====
import { useCallback, useState } from "react";
import { ActivityIndicator, FlatList, Pressable, RefreshControl, StyleSheet, Text, View } from "react-native";
import { SafeAreaView } from "react-native-safe-area-context";
import { Link, useFocusEffect, useLocalSearchParams } from "expo-router";
import { listExploitations, type Exploitation } from "@/services/exploitationservice";
import { labourCosts, listEmployees, type Employee, type LabourCosts } from "@/services/teamService";

export default function TeamsScreen() {
  const { id: exploitationIdParam } = useLocalSearchParams<{ id?: string }>();
  const [exploitations, setExploitations] = useState<Exploitation[]>([]); const [selected, setSelected] = useState<number | null>(exploitationIdParam ? Number(exploitationIdParam) : null); const [employees, setEmployees] = useState<Employee[]>([]); const [costs, setCosts] = useState<LabourCosts | null>(null); const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true); const month = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 7);
  const load = useCallback(async () => { setLoading(true); const farms = await listExploitations({ limit: 100 }); if (farms.success) { setExploitations(farms.data); const requestedId = exploitationIdParam ? Number(exploitationIdParam) : null; const id = requestedId ?? selected ?? farms.data[0]?.id ?? null; setSelected(id); if (id) { const [staff, summary] = await Promise.all([listEmployees(id), labourCosts(id, month)]); if (staff.success) setEmployees(staff.data); if (summary.success) setCosts(summary.data); } } setLoading(false); }, [exploitationIdParam, month, selected]);
  useFocusEffect(useCallback(() => { void load(); }, [load]));
  async function choose(id: number) { setSelected(id); const [staff, summary] = await Promise.all([listEmployees(id), labourCosts(id, month)]); if (staff.success) setEmployees(staff.data); if (summary.success) setCosts(summary.data); }
  return <SafeAreaView style={styles.safe}><View style={styles.container}><Text style={styles.title}>Équipes & salariés</Text><Text style={styles.subtitle}>Contrats, planning, heures et coûts</Text><View style={styles.farms}>{exploitations.map((farm) => <Pressable key={farm.id} onPress={() => choose(farm.id)} style={[styles.farm, selected === farm.id && styles.farmActive]}><Text style={[styles.farmText, selected === farm.id && styles.farmTextActive]}>{farm.name}</Text></Pressable>)}</View>{costs && <View style={styles.costCard}><Text style={styles.costLabel}>Coût de main-d’œuvre · {month}</Text><Text style={styles.costValue}>{costs.totalCost.toFixed(2)} MAD</Text><Text style={styles.costHours}>{costs.totalHours} h déclarées</Text></View>}{loading ? <ActivityIndicator style={{ marginTop: 30 }} /> : <FlatList data={employees} keyExtractor={(item) => String(item.id)} refreshControl={<RefreshControl refreshing={loading} onRefresh={load} />} ListEmptyComponent={<Text style={styles.empty}>Aucun salarié enregistré.</Text>} renderItem={({ item }) => <View style={styles.employee}><View style={styles.avatar}><Text style={styles.avatarText}>{item.firstName[0]}{item.lastName[0]}</Text></View><View><Text style={styles.name}>{item.firstName} {item.lastName}</Text><Text style={styles.position}>{item.position} · {item.status === "ACTIVE" ? "Actif" : "Inactif"}</Text></View></View>} ListFooterComponent={selected ? <Link href={{ pathname: "/exploitations/[id]/teams/create", params: { id: String(selected) } } as any} asChild><Pressable style={styles.add}><Text style={styles.addText}>+ Ajouter un salarié</Text></Pressable></Link> : null} />}</View></SafeAreaView>;
}
const styles = StyleSheet.create({ safe: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: "#f8fafc" }, container: { flex: 1, padding: 16 }, title: { fontSize: 22, fontWeight: "700", color: "#14532d" }, subtitle: { color: "#64748b", marginTop: 3, marginBottom: 14 }, farms: { flexDirection: "row", flexWrap: "wrap", gap: 8, marginBottom: 12 }, farm: { backgroundColor: "#fff", borderWidth: 1, borderColor: "#d1d5db", borderRadius: 20, paddingHorizontal: 12, paddingVertical: 7 }, farmActive: { backgroundColor: "#166534", borderColor: "#166534" }, farmText: { fontSize: 12, color: "#374151" }, farmTextActive: { color: "#fff", fontWeight: "700" }, costCard: { backgroundColor: "#dcfce7", borderRadius: 14, padding: 15, marginBottom: 14 }, costLabel: { color: "#166534", fontSize: 12 }, costValue: { color: "#14532d", fontSize: 24, fontWeight: "800", marginTop: 3 }, costHours: { color: "#166534", fontSize: 12, marginTop: 2 }, employee: { flexDirection: "row", alignItems: "center", backgroundColor: "#fff", padding: 14, borderRadius: 12, marginBottom: 9 }, avatar: { width: 38, height: 38, borderRadius: 19, backgroundColor: "#15803d", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center", marginRight: 11 }, avatarText: { color: "#fff", fontWeight: "700" }, name: { fontWeight: "700", color: "#1f2937" }, position: { color: "#6b7280", fontSize: 12, marginTop: 2 }, empty: { color: "#6b7280", textAlign: "center", marginTop: 32 }, add: { borderWidth: 1.5, borderStyle: "dashed", borderColor: "#15803d", borderRadius: 12, padding: 18, alignItems: "center", marginTop: 6 }, addText: { color: "#166534", fontWeight: "700" } });
===== mobile/src/app/(dashboard)/exploitations/index.tsx =====
import { useCallback, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import {
  View,
  Text,
  FlatList,
  TextInput,
  Pressable,
  Image,
  StyleSheet,
  ActivityIndicator,
  RefreshControl,
} from "react-native";
import { SafeAreaView } from "react-native-safe-area-context";
import { Link, useFocusEffect } from "expo-router";
import {
  listExploitations,
  type Exploitation,
} from "../../../services/exploitationservice";
import {
  EXPLOITATION_TYPES,
  getExploitationTypeInfo,
} from "../../../constants/exploitationTypes";
import { BackButton } from "../../../components/BackButton";

// ADAPTER : remplacez par la vraie baseURL utilisee dans services/api.ts
const API_ORIGIN = "http://172.27.182.10:3000";

type FilterType = "TOUT" | "OVIN" | "CAPRIN" | "MIXTE";

export default function ExploitationsListScreen() {
  const [exploitations, setExploitations] = useState<Exploitation[]>([]);
  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
  const [filter, setFilter] = useState<FilterType>("TOUT");
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [refreshing, setRefreshing] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  async function fetchExploitations() {
    setError(null);
    const result = await listExploitations({ search: search || undefined, limit: 50 });
    if (result.success) {
      setExploitations(result.data);
    } else {
      setError(result.message);
    }
  }

  useFocusEffect(
    useCallback(() => {
      setLoading(true);
      fetchExploitations().finally(() => setLoading(false));
    }, [])
  );

  async function onRefresh() {
    setRefreshing(true);
    await fetchExploitations();
    setRefreshing(false);
  }

  const filteredExploitations = useMemo(() => {
    if (filter === "TOUT") return exploitations;
    return exploitations.filter((e) => e.type === filter);
  }, [exploitations, filter]);

  return (
    <SafeAreaView style={styles.safeArea} edges={["top", "bottom"]}>
      <View style={styles.container}>
        {/* Header with Back Button */}
        <View style={styles.headerRow}>
          <BackButton variant="dark" style={styles.backButton} />
          <View style={styles.headerTitleContainer}>
            <Text style={styles.title}>Mes exploitations</Text>
            <Text style={styles.subtitle}>
              {filteredExploitations.length} exploitation
              {filteredExploitations.length > 1 ? "s" : ""}
            </Text>
          </View>
        </View>

        <TextInput
          style={styles.searchInput}
          placeholder="Rechercher une ferme..."
          placeholderTextColor="#999"
          value={search}
          onChangeText={setSearch}
          onSubmitEditing={() => {
            setLoading(true);
            fetchExploitations().finally(() => setLoading(false));
          }}
        />

        <View style={styles.filterRow}>
          <FilterPill
            label="Tout"
            active={filter === "TOUT"}
            onPress={() => setFilter("TOUT")}
          />
          {EXPLOITATION_TYPES.map((t) => (
            <FilterPill
              key={t.id}
              label={t.label}
              active={filter === t.id}
              onPress={() => setFilter(t.id)}
            />
          ))}
        </View>

        {error && <Text style={styles.error}>{error}</Text>}

        {loading ? (
          <ActivityIndicator style={{ marginTop: 24 }} />
        ) : (
          <FlatList
            data={filteredExploitations}
            keyExtractor={(item) => String(item.id)}
            contentContainerStyle={styles.listContent}
            refreshControl={
              <RefreshControl refreshing={refreshing} onRefresh={onRefresh} />
            }
            ListEmptyComponent={
              <Text style={styles.empty}>Aucune exploitation trouvee.</Text>
            }
            ListFooterComponent={
              <Link href={"/exploitations/create" as any} asChild>
                <Pressable style={styles.addCard}>
                  <View style={styles.addIconCircle}>
                    <Text style={styles.addIcon}>+</Text>
                  </View>
                  <Text style={styles.addCardText}>Ajouter une exploitation</Text>
                </Pressable>
              </Link>
            }
            renderItem={({ item }) => {
              const typeInfo = getExploitationTypeInfo(item.type);
              const hasCoords = item.latitude && item.longitude;

              return (
                <Link
                  href={
                    {
                      pathname: "/exploitations/[id]",
                      params: { id: String(item.id) },
                    } as any
                  }
                  asChild
                >
                  <Pressable style={styles.card}>
                    <View style={styles.photoWrapper}>
                      {item.photo ? (
                        <Image
                          source={{ uri: `${API_ORIGIN}${item.photo}` }}
                          style={styles.photo}
                        />
                      ) : (
                        <View style={[styles.photo, styles.photoPlaceholder]}>
                          <Text style={styles.photoPlaceholderIcon}>
                            {typeInfo.icon}
                          </Text>
                        </View>
                      )}

                      <View
                        style={[
                          styles.typeBadge,
                          { backgroundColor: typeInfo.color },
                        ]}
                      >
                        <Text style={styles.typeBadgeIcon}>{typeInfo.icon}</Text>
                        <Text style={styles.typeBadgeText}>{typeInfo.label}</Text>
                      </View>
                    </View>

                    <View style={styles.cardBody}>
                      <Text style={styles.name}>{item.name}</Text>

                      <View style={styles.metaGrid}>
                        <View style={styles.metaItem}>
                          <Text style={styles.metaIcon}>📍</Text>
                          <Text style={styles.metaText} numberOfLines={1}>
                            {hasCoords
                              ? `${Number(item.latitude).toFixed(2)}, ${Number(
                                  item.longitude
                                ).toFixed(2)}`
                              : "GPS non renseigne"}
                          </Text>
                        </View>
                        <View style={styles.metaItem}>
                          <Text style={styles.metaIcon}>📐</Text>
                          <Text style={styles.metaText}>
                            {item.superficie ? `${item.superficie} ha` : "—"}
                          </Text>
                        </View>
                      </View>

                      <Text style={styles.dateText}>
                        Creee le{" "}
                        {new Date(item.createdAt).toLocaleDateString("fr-FR")}
                      </Text>
                    </View>
                  </Pressable>
                </Link>
              );
            }}
          />
        )}
      </View>
    </SafeAreaView>
  );
}

function FilterPill({
  label,
  active,
  onPress,
}: {
  label: string;
  active: boolean;
  onPress: () => void;
}) {
  return (
    <Pressable
      onPress={onPress}
      style={[styles.filterPill, active && styles.filterPillActive]}
    >
      <Text style={[styles.filterPillText, active && styles.filterPillTextActive]}>
        {label}
      </Text>
    </Pressable>
  );
}

});===== mobile/src/app/(dashboard)/health/report.tsx =====
import { useCallback, useState } from "react";
import {
  View,
  Text,
  ScrollView,
  Pressable,
  StyleSheet,
  ActivityIndicator,
  RefreshControl,
} from "react-native";
import { SafeAreaView } from "react-native-safe-area-context";
import { useRouter, useFocusEffect } from "expo-router";
import { Ionicons } from "@expo/vector-icons";
import api from "../../../services/api";

interface ReportData {
  summary: {
    totalAnimals: number;
    totalHealthRecords: number;
    morbidityRate: number;
    mortalityRate: number;
    avgCostPerAnimal: number;
    avgRecoveryDays: number;
  };
  statusDistribution: Record<string, number>;
  recentActivities: Array<{
    type: string;
    date: string;
    description: string;
  }>;
}

const statusConfig: Record<string, { label: string; color: string; bg: string; dot: string }> = {
  HEALTHY:        { label: 'Sain',          color: '#059669', bg: '#ECFDF5', dot: '#10B981' },
  SURVEILLANCE:   { label: 'Surveillance',  color: '#D97706', bg: '#FFFBEB', dot: '#F59E0B' },
  SICK:           { label: 'Malade',        color: '#DC2626', bg: '#FEF2F2', dot: '#EF4444' },
  UNDER_TREATMENT:{ label: 'En traitement', color: '#EA580C', bg: '#FFEDD5', dot: '#F97316' },
  RECOVERED:      { label: 'Rétabli',       color: '#2563EB', bg: '#EFF6FF', dot: '#3B82F6' },
  PENDING:        { label: 'En attente',    color: '#D97706', bg: '#FFFBEB', dot: '#F59E0B' },
  DONE:           { label: 'Effectué',      color: '#059669', bg: '#ECFDF5', dot: '#10B981' },
  OVERDUE:        { label: 'En retard',     color: '#DC2626', bg: '#FEF2F2', dot: '#EF4444' },
};

export default function HealthReportScreen() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [report, setReport] = useState<ReportData | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [refreshing, setRefreshing] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const fetchReport = useCallback(async () => {
    setError(null);
    try {
      const response = await api.get("/health/reports/summary");
      setReport(response.data.data);
    } catch (err) {
      setError("Erreur de chargement du rapport");
      console.error(err);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, []);

  useFocusEffect(
    useCallback(() => {
      setLoading(true);
      fetchReport();
    }, [fetchReport])
  );

  const onRefresh = useCallback(async () => {
    setRefreshing(true);
    await fetchReport();
    setRefreshing(false);
  }, [fetchReport]);

  const getStatusInfo = (status: string) => {
    return statusConfig[status] || {
      label: status,
      color: '#64748B',
      bg: '#F8FAFC',
      dot: '#94A3B8',
    };
  };

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <SafeAreaView style={styles.safeArea} edges={["top", "bottom"]}>
        <View style={styles.loadingContainer}>
          <ActivityIndicator size="large" color="#10B981" />
          <Text style={styles.loadingText}>Génération du rapport…</Text>
        </View>
      </SafeAreaView>
    );
  }

  if (error || !report) {
    return (
      <SafeAreaView style={styles.safeArea} edges={["top", "bottom"]}>
        <View style={styles.header}>
          <Pressable
            onPress={() => router.back()}
            style={({ pressed }) => [styles.backBtn, pressed && styles.btnPressed]}
            hitSlop={12}
          >
            <Ionicons name="arrow-back" size={18} color="#0F172A" />
          </Pressable>
          <Text style={styles.headerTitle}>Rapport sanitaire</Text>
          <View style={{ width: 40 }} />
        </View>
        <View style={styles.centerContainer}>
          <View style={styles.errorBanner}>
            <Text style={styles.errorText}>⚠️ {error ?? "Rapport introuvable."}</Text>
          </View>
        </View>
      </SafeAreaView>
    );
  }

  const { summary, statusDistribution, recentActivities } = report;
  const totalAnimals = summary.totalAnimals || 1;

  const kpiItems = [
    { label: "Total Animaux", value: summary.totalAnimals, sub: "Tête(s) enregistrée(s)", icon: "🐑" },
    { label: "Dossiers Médicaux", value: summary.totalHealthRecords, sub: "Consultations", icon: "📂" },
    { label: "Taux de Morbidité", value: `${summary.morbidityRate}%`, sub: "Cas détectés", icon: "🤒" },
    { label: "Taux de Mortalité", value: `${summary.mortalityRate}%`, sub: "Pertes enregistrées", icon: "⚠️" },
    { label: "Coût Moyen / Animal", value: `${summary.avgCostPerAnimal.toFixed(2)} MAD`, sub: "Dépenses de santé", icon: "💰" },
    { label: "Durée de Guérison", value: `${summary.avgRecoveryDays} j`, sub: "Moyenne estimée", icon: "⏱️" },
  ];

  return (
    <SafeAreaView style={styles.safeArea} edges={["top", "bottom"]}>
      <View style={styles.header}>
        <Pressable
          onPress={() => router.back()}
          style={({ pressed }) => [styles.backBtn, pressed && styles.btnPressed]}
          hitSlop={12}
        >
          <Ionicons name="arrow-back" size={18} color="#0F172A" />
        </Pressable>
        <View style={styles.headerTitleBlock}>
          <Text style={styles.headerTag}>ANALYTIQUE</Text>
          <Text style={styles.headerTitle}>Rapport sanitaire</Text>
        </View>
        <View style={{ width: 40 }} />
      </View>

      <ScrollView
        contentContainerStyle={styles.container}
        showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
        refreshControl={
          <RefreshControl
            refreshing={refreshing}
            onRefresh={onRefresh}
            tintColor="#10B981"
            colors={["#10B981"]}
          />
        }
      >
        <View style={styles.kpiGrid}>
          {kpiItems.map((kpi, idx) => (
            <View key={idx} style={styles.kpiCard}>
              <View style={styles.kpiCardTop}>
                <Text style={styles.kpiIcon}>{kpi.icon}</Text>
                <Text style={styles.kpiLabel}>{kpi.label}</Text>
              </View>
              <Text style={styles.kpiValue}>{kpi.value}</Text>
              <Text style={styles.kpiSub}>{kpi.sub}</Text>
            </View>
          ))}
        </View>

        <View style={styles.sectionCard}>
          <View style={styles.sectionHeader}>
            <Text style={styles.sectionTitle}>Répartition des statuts</Text>
            <Text style={styles.sectionBadge}>En direct</Text>
          </View>

          {Object.keys(statusDistribution).length === 0 ? (
            <Text style={styles.emptyText}>Aucune donnée disponible</Text>
          ) : (
            Object.entries(statusDistribution).map(([status, count]) => {
              const info = getStatusInfo(status);
              const percentage = Math.round((count / totalAnimals) * 100);

              return (
                <View key={status} style={styles.statusRow}>
                  <View style={styles.statusHeaderRow}>
                    <View style={styles.statusLabelGroup}>
                      <View style={[styles.statusDot, { backgroundColor: info.dot }]} />
                      <Text style={styles.statusLabelText}>{info.label}</Text>
                    </View>
                    <View style={styles.statusValueGroup}>
                      <Text style={styles.statusCountText}>{count}</Text>
                      <Text style={styles.statusPercentageText}>({percentage}%)</Text>
                    </View>
                  </View>
                  <View style={styles.progressBarBg}>
                    <View
                      style={[
                        styles.progressBarFill,
                        {
                          width: `${Math.min(percentage, 100)}%`,
                          backgroundColor: info.color,
                        },
                      ]}
                    />
                  </View>
                </View>
              );
            })
          )}
        </View>

        <View style={styles.sectionCard}>
          <View style={styles.sectionHeader}>
            <Text style={styles.sectionTitle}>Dernières activités</Text>
            <Text style={styles.sectionSubCount}>{recentActivities.length} récents</Text>
          </View>

          {recentActivities.length === 0 ? (
            <Text style={styles.emptyText}>Aucune activité récente</Text>
          ) : (
            recentActivities.map((activity, index) => {
              const icon =
                activity.type === "health_record"
                  ? "🏥"
                  : activity.type === "treatment"
                  ? "💊"
                  : "💉";

              const isLast = index === recentActivities.length - 1;

              return (
                <View
                  key={index}
                  style={[styles.activityItem, isLast && styles.activityItemLast]}
                >
                  <View style={styles.activityAvatar}>
                    <Text style={{ fontSize: 16 }}>{icon}</Text>
                  </View>
                  <View style={styles.activityContent}>
                    <Text style={styles.activityDescription}>{activity.description}</Text>
                    <Text style={styles.activityDate}>
                      {new Date(activity.date).toLocaleDateString("fr-FR", {
                        day: "2-digit",
                        month: "short",
                        year: "numeric",
                        hour: "2-digit",
                        minute: "2-digit",
                      })}
                    </Text>
                  </View>
                </View>
              );
            })
          )}
        </View>
      </ScrollView>
    </SafeAreaView>
  );
}

// ─── Styles ────────────────────────────────────────────────────

});===== mobile/src/app/(dashboard)/health/create.tsx =====
import { useState, useEffect } from "react";
import {
  View,
  Text,
  TextInput,
  Pressable,
  StyleSheet,
  ActivityIndicator,
  ScrollView,
  KeyboardAvoidingView,
  Platform,
  Alert,
  FlatList,
} from "react-native";
import { SafeAreaView } from "react-native-safe-area-context";
import { useRouter } from "expo-router";
import { Ionicons, Feather } from "@expo/vector-icons";
import api from "../../../services/api";
import { BackButton } from "../../../components/BackButton";

const STATUSES = [
  { id: "HEALTHY", label: "Sain", icon: "✅" },
  { id: "SURVEILLANCE", label: "Surveillance", icon: "👀" },
  { id: "SICK", label: "Malade", icon: "🤒" },
  { id: "UNDER_TREATMENT", label: "En traitement", icon: "💊" },
  { id: "RECOVERED", label: "Rétabli", icon: "💪" },
];

const SEVERITIES = [
  { id: "LOW", label: "Faible", color: "#16a34a" },
  { id: "MEDIUM", label: "Moyenne", color: "#ca8a04" },
  { id: "HIGH", label: "Élevée", color: "#ea580c" },
  { id: "CRITICAL", label: "Critique", color: "#dc2626" },
];

export default function CreateHealthRecord() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [animals, setAnimals] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [filteredAnimals, setFilteredAnimals] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [loadingAnimals, setLoadingAnimals] = useState(true);
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState("");
  const [selectedAnimal, setSelectedAnimal] = useState<any | null>(null);

  const [form, setForm] = useState({
    animalId: "",
    status: "HEALTHY",
    symptoms: "",
    diagnosis: "",
    severity: "LOW",
  });
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // Charger la liste des animaux une fois
  useEffect(() => {
    api.get("/animals")
      .then(res => {
        setAnimals(res.data.data);
        setLoadingAnimals(false);
      })
      .catch(() => setLoadingAnimals(false));
  }, []);

  // Filtrer les animaux en fonction de la recherche (RFID ou nom)
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!searchQuery.trim()) {
      setFilteredAnimals([]);
      return;
    }
    const query = searchQuery.toLowerCase().trim();
    const filtered = animals.filter((a) =>
      a.rfid.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
      a.name.toLowerCase().includes(query)
    );
    setFilteredAnimals(filtered);
  }, [searchQuery, animals]);

  // Sélectionner un animal
  const selectAnimal = (animal: any) => {
    setSelectedAnimal(animal);
    setForm({ ...form, animalId: String(animal.id) });
    setSearchQuery(animal.rfid); // Affiche le RFID dans la barre
    setFilteredAnimals([]); // Cache la liste
  };

  // Effacer la sélection
  const clearSelection = () => {
    setSelectedAnimal(null);
    setForm({ ...form, animalId: "" });
    setSearchQuery("");
    setFilteredAnimals([]);
  };

  function validate(): string | null {
    if (!form.animalId) return "Veuillez sélectionner un animal.";
    return null;
  }

  async function handleSubmit() {
    const validationError = validate();
    if (validationError) {
      setError(validationError);
      return;
    }

    setLoading(true);
    setError(null);

    try {
      await api.post("/health/records", {
        animalId: Number(form.animalId),
        status: form.status,
        symptoms: form.symptoms || undefined,
        diagnosis: form.diagnosis || undefined,
        severity: form.severity,
      });
      Alert.alert("Succès", "Dossier médical créé");
      router.replace("/health");
    } catch (err) {
      setError("Erreur lors de la création");
      console.error(err);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <SafeAreaView style={styles.safeArea} edges={["top", "bottom"]}>
      <KeyboardAvoidingView
        style={{ flex: 1 }}
        behavior={Platform.OS === "ios" ? "padding" : undefined}
      >
        {/* Header */}
        <View style={styles.header}>
          <BackButton variant="dark" style={styles.backButton} />
          <Text style={styles.headerTitle}>Nouveau dossier médical</Text>
          <View style={styles.headerAvatar}>
            <Ionicons name="medical" size={18} color="#fff" />
          </View>
        </View>

        <ScrollView
          contentContainerStyle={styles.container}
          keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled"
        >
          {/* 1. Recherche et sélection de l'animal */}
          <View style={styles.fieldGroup}>
            <Text style={styles.label}>Rechercher un animal par RFID ou nom</Text>
            <View style={styles.searchWrapper}>
              <Ionicons name="search-outline" size={20} color="#6B7280" />
              <TextInput
                style={styles.searchInput}
                placeholder="Ex: MA202600001245 ou Shahin"
                placeholderTextColor="#9CA3AF"
                value={searchQuery}
                onChangeText={setSearchQuery}
              />
              {selectedAnimal && (
                <Pressable onPress={clearSelection} style={styles.clearButton}>
                  <Ionicons name="close-circle" size={20} color="#9CA3AF" />
                </Pressable>
              )}
            </View>

            {/* Liste des résultats */}
            {filteredAnimals.length > 0 && (
              <View style={styles.resultsContainer}>
                <FlatList
                  data={filteredAnimals}
                  keyExtractor={(item) => String(item.id)}
                  renderItem={({ item }) => (
                    <Pressable
                      style={styles.resultItem}
                      onPress={() => selectAnimal(item)}
                    >
                      <Text style={styles.resultRfid}>{item.rfid}</Text>
                      <Text style={styles.resultName}>{item.name}</Text>
                    </Pressable>
                  )}
                  keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled"
                />
              </View>
            )}

            {/* Animal sélectionné */}
            {selectedAnimal && (
              <View style={styles.selectedCard}>
                <View style={styles.selectedContent}>
                  <Text style={styles.selectedIcon}>🐑</Text>
                  <View style={styles.selectedInfo}>
                    <Text style={styles.selectedName}>{selectedAnimal.name}</Text>
                    <Text style={styles.selectedRfid}>{selectedAnimal.rfid}</Text>
                  </View>
                </View>
                <Pressable onPress={clearSelection} style={styles.selectedClear}>
                  <Text style={styles.selectedClearText}>✕</Text>
                </Pressable>
              </View>
            )}
          </View>

          {/* 2. Statut (compact) */}
          <SectionTitle index={2} label="Statut" />
          <View style={styles.fieldGroup}>
            <View style={styles.optionsRowSmall}>
              {STATUSES.map((s) => {
                const selected = form.status === s.id;
                return (
                  <Pressable
                    key={s.id}
                    onPress={() => setForm({ ...form, status: s.id })}
                    style={[styles.optionChipSmall, selected && styles.optionChipSelectedSmall]}
                  >
                    <Text style={[styles.optionChipIconSmall, selected && { color: "#fff" }]}>
                      {s.icon}
                    </Text>
                    <Text style={[styles.optionChipLabelSmall, selected && { color: "#fff" }]}>
                      {s.label}
                    </Text>
                  </Pressable>
                );
              })}
            </View>
          </View>

          {/* 3. Gravité (compact) */}
          <SectionTitle index={3} label="Gravité" />
          <View style={styles.fieldGroup}>
            <View style={styles.optionsRowSmall}>
              {SEVERITIES.map((s) => {
                const selected = form.severity === s.id;
                return (
                  <Pressable
                    key={s.id}
                    onPress={() => setForm({ ...form, severity: s.id })}
                    style={[
                      styles.optionChipSmall,
                      selected && { backgroundColor: s.color, borderColor: s.color },
                    ]}
                  >
                    <Text style={[styles.optionChipLabelSmall, selected && { color: "#fff" }]}>
                      {s.label}
                    </Text>
                  </Pressable>
                );
              })}
            </View>
          </View>

          {/* 4. Symptômes */}
          <SectionTitle index={4} label="Symptômes" />
          <View style={styles.fieldGroup}>
            <TextInput
              style={styles.textArea}
              placeholder="Décrivez les symptômes..."
              multiline
              numberOfLines={4}
              value={form.symptoms}
              onChangeText={(text) => setForm({ ...form, symptoms: text })}
            />
          </View>

          {/* 5. Diagnostic */}
          <SectionTitle index={5} label="Diagnostic" />
          <View style={styles.fieldGroup}>
            <TextInput
              style={styles.textArea}
              placeholder="Diagnostic..."
              multiline
              numberOfLines={3}
              value={form.diagnosis}
              onChangeText={(text) => setForm({ ...form, diagnosis: text })}
            />
          </View>

          {error && <Text style={styles.error}>{error}</Text>}

          <View style={styles.actionsRow}>
            <Pressable style={styles.cancelButton} onPress={() => router.back()}>
              <Text style={styles.cancelButtonText}>ANNULER</Text>
            </Pressable>
            <Pressable style={styles.submitButton} onPress={handleSubmit} disabled={loading}>
              {loading ? (
                <ActivityIndicator color="#fff" />
              ) : (
                <>
                  <Feather name="save" size={16} color="#fff" style={{ marginRight: 8 }} />
                  <Text style={styles.submitButtonText}>ENREGISTRER</Text>
                </>
              )}
            </Pressable>
          </View>
        </ScrollView>
      </KeyboardAvoidingView>
    </SafeAreaView>
  );
}

function SectionTitle({ index, label }: { index: number; label: string }) {
  return (
    <View style={styles.sectionTitleRow}>
      <View style={styles.sectionBar} />
      <Text style={styles.sectionTitle}>
        {index}. {label}
      </Text>
    </View>
  );
}

const BORDER = "#e5e0d8";

  safeArea: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: "#faf6f1" },

  optionsRowSmall: {

    flexWrap: "wrap",
    gap: 6,
    marginBottom: 4,
  },
  optionChipSmall: {
    flex: 1,
    minWidth: "18%",
    alignItems: "center",
    justifyContent: "center",

  // Champs texte
  textArea: {

  actionsRow: { flexDirection: "row", gap: 10, marginTop: 10 },

    flexDirection: "row",
    backgroundColor: GREEN,

    paddingVertical: 12,
    alignItems: "center",
